- **review-falsifier**: Spec-anchored falsification by execution — the battery's only lens that RUNS the change instead of reading it. Catches: implemented-the-wrong-requirement, promises that hold in tests but not through the real CLI entry path, and the defect class reading cannot see. - **review-judge**: Read-only blind judge for temper and hone rounds. - **review-states**: Reviews one dimension — every view handles empty, loading, partial, error and success, and every action gives feedback. Read-only. ([neolao/claude-plugin-ux](https://github.com/neolao/claude-plugin-ux/blob/HEAD/agents/review-states.md))
- **review-steward**: Owns the maintenance and evolution of the 226os AI review pipeline (stage 1 review and deep review, ledger, Review gate, auto-fix, auto-merge, batch, settle, findings issues; ADR-0013, ADR-0014, ADR-0015). Use for any change, incident, review follow-up or ADR-0015 phase on scripts/review-*.py, scripts/findings.py, .github/workflows/code-review.yml and review-*.yml, .github/*-paths.txt, .claude/commands/review-fix.md, deep-review.md, delegate-review.md and their tests. Requires an owner-approved task with allowed paths and a stop condition. ([Y4NN777/226os](https://github.com/Y4NN777/226os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-steward.md) · ★2)
- **review-structuralist**: Architecture review agent — evaluates structural coherence, boundary placement, evolution fitness, and over-engineering at design, plan, and code levels. ([cspergel/forgedev](https://github.com/cspergel/forgedev/blob/HEAD/agents/review-structuralist.md))
- **review-structure**: Reviewer for one model-onboarding PR. Checks reuse of existing helpers and templates, duplication, dead code, class structure, and use of repo abstractions such as external_repos. Launched by the model-onboarding-review skill (Lens I). ([qualcomm/ai-hub-models](https://github.com/qualcomm/ai-hub-models/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/review-structure.md) · ★1.3k)
- **review-structure**: Reviews code changes for over-engineering and fixes implemented at the wrong abstraction level. ([synesenom/ran](https://github.com/synesenom/ran/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-structure.md) · ★15)
- **review-structure**: Рецензент скіла review — структура документа як цілого. Звіряє зміст із заголовками частин, додатки й реєстри питань із частинами, що їх підняли, розділ джерел із посиланнями, терміни зі словником проєкту, коди й скорочення з реєстром кодів. Чи зрозуміє документ інший інженер без автора, оцінює за вступом і змістом. Файлів не змінює. Викликає скіл review. ([alexgorby/claude-skills](https://github.com/alexgorby/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/ukrainian-tech-docs/agents/review-structure.md) · ★6)
- **review-structure**: Aspect reviewer for human-primary articles and essays — structure only. Checks that one central thesis is carried through the causal spine, each section has one role and one discovery, no independent argument rides along (overload), sections do not repeat each other, and the ending is the author's judgment. Dispatched by `writing-ecosystem` §4 once per draft, alongside the other aspect reviewers. NOT for — certainty against the author's words (review-certainty), explanations and examples (review-explanation), prose and terms (review-prose), conventions (review-conventions), facts (fact-checke… ([shimo4228/writing](https://github.com/shimo4228/writing/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-structure.md) · ★3)
- **review-structure-nextjs**: Use this agent on the current diff (uncommitted or last commit) to audit ONLY structural concerns — file placement under src/app vs src/features vs src/components vs src/lib, route segment conventions (page/layout/error/loading), file naming (kebab-case vs PascalCase), import path conventions (@/ alias), feature isolation (no cross-feature imports), and bundle boundary correctness (server-only modules in client bundles). Runs IN PARALLEL with review-solid-nextjs and review-ux-nextjs. Read-only. Produces a severity-sorted report tagged [structure]. ([QuentinDoniczka/portfolio](https://github.com/QuentinDoniczka/portfolio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-structure-nextjs.md))
- **review-structure.agent**: コード構造・設計・命名・型安全性のレビューを担当するエージェント ([ako-fa/copilot-chat-customizations](https://github.com/ako-fa/copilot-chat-customizations/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/review-structure.agent.md))
- **review-style**: Single-axis style and coding-convention review of a diff or component, dispatching to the KJ/C++, Rust or TypeScript guide by file type. Returns findings only, in the shared finding format. Invoked by the code-review agent's review fan-out; not useful on its own. ([cloudflare/workerd](https://github.com/cloudflare/workerd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-style.md) · ★9.2k)
- **review-style**: Review-team lens: project conventions the linters don't enforce — naming, module boundaries, duplicated helpers, CLAUDE.md rules. Used by the review-team workflow; one branch per call. ([badetitou/badetitou-team](https://github.com/badetitou/badetitou-team/blob/HEAD/agents/review-style.md))
- **review-style**: Style and quality reviewer — checks code consistency, naming conventions, readability, test quality, and dead code in local code changes. ([jerrod/agent-plugins](https://github.com/jerrod/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/agents/review-style.md))
- **review-style**: 可読性・規約レビュー専用subagent。差分だけを読み、命名・可読性・プロジェクト規約への準拠を確認してseverity付きで報告する。PROACTIVELY delegate when 実装完了後・MR作成前・「レビューして」と依頼されたとき。codex-ext:gitlab-develop・codex-ext:gitlab-reviewから4並列の1本として起動される。 ([Sylphy0052/VSCode-Codex-Extension](https://github.com/Sylphy0052/VSCode-Codex-Extension/blob/HEAD/resources/skills-plugin/agents/review-style.md))
- **review-sub**: 리뷰 Sub-Agent — 코드 리뷰, 보안 검토 (Write/Edit 불가) ([AI-Agent-Jeon/ClaudeManager-v2](https://github.com/AI-Agent-Jeon/ClaudeManager-v2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-sub.md))
- **review-subagent**: Use for the read-only "Review-Subagent" role in agent/skills/pre-review/SKILL.md — applies the lane checklists in agent/reviews/pre-review.md against a diff/blast-radius the caller supplies (scan.py output, changed paths, active spec), and returns findings JSON. Never edits files; does not decide fixes, run script/qa, or run any git/shell command. ([ibes/emsig](https://github.com/ibes/emsig/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-subagent.md))
- **review-subtractive**: 「この diff は足さずに済まないか」を問う観点別レビュアー。diff のモード（implementation/documentation-workflow/mixed）ごとに削減・統合の可能性を検証する。他系統より先（入口）に起動し、周回上限に達したときの「反復・point-fix」分類でも戻り先になる。pre-commit-review / review-pr のレビューループで使用する。 ([clockcrockwork/novel-ide-code](https://github.com/clockcrockwork/novel-ide-code/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-subtractive.md))
- **review-summarizer**: Combines findings from all review agents into a single actionable code review summary with an overall verdict. ([openmotus/motus](https://github.com/openmotus/motus/blob/HEAD/examples/code-review/agents/review-summarizer.md) · ★4)
- **review-summarizer**: Aggregates findings from all review specialists into a unified PR review comment. Read-only. ([ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize](https://github.com/ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-summarizer.md) · 2 copies)
- **review-summarizer**: Drafts the summary of a batch of game reviews - what each bot does well and badly in each phase, the decisive moments that recur, and which metrics go with winning - from the reviews and the batch's computed summary (or from a metrics report of a large batch alone), and returns it as its reply for the session to save. Use through the review-games skill, after the game-reviewer agents; it runs on Haiku so that reviews stay cheap. ([Arthur-Vat/codingame-bots](https://github.com/Arthur-Vat/codingame-bots/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-summarizer.md))
- **review-summary**: Blind summarizer for the Stilus review phase. ([flexion/claude-domestique](https://github.com/flexion/claude-domestique/blob/HEAD/stilus/agents/review-summary.md) · ★1)
- **review-summary-writer**: Merges findings from several reviewers (human or agent: correctness, readability, test adequacy, risk) into one deduplicated, prioritised review with a single verdict. Hand work to it after parallel reviews finish and before posting a review on a PR. ([posterus-ltd/yarkr-public](https://github.com/posterus-ltd/yarkr-public/blob/HEAD/agents/review-summary-writer.md))
- **review-summary-writer**: 1. Receber findings de todos os revisores 2. Deduplicar findings sobrepostos entre revisores 3. Agrupar por categoria (segurança, lógica, arquitetura, estilo) 4. Priorizar por severidade (CRITICAL &gt; HIGH &gt; MEDIUM &gt; LOW &gt; INFO) 5. Sugerir fix para cada finding 6. Reconhecer pontos positivos do código 7. Definir verdict (APPROVE / REQUEST_CHANGES / BLOCK) 8. Formatar summary no formato solicitado 9. ([wesleyschons/squads-sh-aios](https://github.com/wesleyschons/squads-sh-aios/blob/HEAD/automated-code-review-squad/agents/review-summary-writer.md))
- **review-supervisor**: Aggregates the four review_team judges' independently-persisted verdicts and reports a consolidated result. Does not spawn or invoke sub-agents. Invoke with a work unit ID (e.g. E0-F1-S1-T1). ([caylent-solutions/devbench](https://github.com/caylent-solutions/devbench/blob/HEAD/plugin/devbench-orchestrate/agents/review-supervisor.md) · ★8)
- **review-surfacer**: Surfacing phase reviewer for the code review pipeline. Evaluates changed code within a specific review dimension, surfaces concerns via MCP tools, and notifies the lead when done. Use proactively when spawning code review teammates.
